## What we found today
- FT healthy cost (online EPLB on, 128/GPU): unresolved at n=4 (+1.93%, CI -1.68..5.53, #112). Profiles: the only consistent
  FT step cost is the MTP layer's receive-slot copy, +122 us/step (~0.45%, #113) - removed in the final wheel.
- Ghost layout (online EPLB off, 320 vs 256 slots, FT off): +6.2% = autotuner tactic choice 5.0% (fix A) + load-balancer
  route path 1.1% (fix B); empty ghost slots ~0 (#110). Fix B verified on GPU; fix A's effect not yet measured.
- hang_confirm_s stays 30 s: 10 s wrongly fenced a GIL-starved rank (#109).
- Host-daemon loss: job survives with a PRRTE patch but MPI_Finalize hangs (#111) - out-of-scope boundary.
- Final-design code passes the GPU integration suite 10/10 (#114, without fix B).
- Corrected today: "fix B breaks dispatch" (false: MPI spawn fails in our containers), "test_moe_comm passed before"
  (never ran), ladder "outside noise" (CI includes 0). Spawn-based tests now run only under mpirun (tools/test_wheel.sh mpi:).

restore, tuning with loaded slots (fix A), route-kernel skip (fix B), MTP in-place receive slots, test fixes. Parked: stream
continuation (phase B). Kept: both heartbeats, hang_confirm_s 30 s, detector 5 s.

If units fail on either added commit, it is dropped from build and PR stack and the build reruns; this protocol then applies to the new SHA.
- Healthy finals: launch/finals_std_cmd.txt F1-F4, 4 jobs x 2 node groups. Unit of replication = node group. Each group runs two EPLB modes, one case per arm, mirrored order (X-off X-on Y-on Y-off or X-on X-off Y-off Y-on); w/o EPLB, offline and online each on 5 groups; F4 G1 adds std-noeplb-lb vs std-noeplb (static-LB path, 256 slots, FT off; not FT's 320 layout).
- Config: std-*.yaml (perf-sanity R1 FP4 recipe at DEP32, documented deviations identical in every arm: max_seq_len 2304, stream_interval 10, 4 postprocess workers, print_iter_log, host_cache_size 16 GiB/rank, TRTLLM_KVCACHE_POOL_USE_FABRIC_MEMORY=0, TLLM_FMHA_LIBS=-cute_dsl_mla, forced MTP acceptance 1, backlog 16384), loads 8/32/128/512 per GPU (>=150 s each), unmeasured burn-in 32/GPU x 6 waves, tactics pinned from launch/std/std-seed2.json, offline placement STD-8109649, WIDEEP_NO_SAMPLERS=1. FT on = ULFM ob1/TCP + detector + authority + FT config (by design; disclosed, not split).
- Primary metric: rank-0 loop-step median per load over the decode-only, full-load window, reported as decode tok/s/GPU; cost = FT on vs FT off within the same group. Test per cell (mode x load): one-sided, upper 95% bound of the mean group-paired cost < +1% (t over groups). A claim "< 1% in every cell" needs every cell to pass; any per-cell list or worst-cell headline is Holm-corrected over the 12 cells. Also reported: TPOT (mean, median), client output tok/s/GPU (descriptive, median over groups with min-max), step mean, stall share (time in steps > 2x median, FT may not add > 0.5 points: Hodges-Lehmann + Wilcoxon), 32/GPU compared within KV-fill bins. 512/GPU shown, marked beyond recipe, not in the headline.
- Exclusions: none, except a case with PIN_CHECK FAIL (tactics not pinned) or a startup failure; such a case is rerun in a later slot. Every started case is reported. A row with > 5% failed or short requests keeps its step metrics, not its client metrics.
- Kills: launch/kills_final_cmd.txt (n=3 per cell, rows interleaved, KF_ITERATIONS=4). Labels: "one rank killed", "all ranks of one node killed (host stays up)"; recovery_s = failure to the first committed step on the survivors (server side); post-loss = "TPOT impact at fixed load". Real host loss: untested beyond 3-node runs (disclosed).

Its H2 (receive CTAs trigger PDL at entry, so routing is resident ~6 us early) is a second path to kernels_a.md's SM-residency H1; its H3 is the slot stride (STRIDE32 running). Caveat: FT replays miss one moeA2APrepareDispatchKernel CUPTI record (layer 32), so windows keyed on PrepareDispatch merge two layers once per step; moe_path.md's FT per-window medians may include them. Key on moeA2ADispatchKernel/graphNodeId.
- 00:12 8/GPU gate design (review/hostfix/gate_c.md): at 8/GPU closed-loop the hold condition can't be met mid-wave, so a straggler just after a burst waits 50-59 iterations; the held user re-arrives mid-wave, which makes the mode bistable. Fix: release after W = ceil(T*ln(1+A)/A) quiet iterations (A = requests routed in the last T iterations; W = T at A = 0). Independent red team running (gate_redteam.md); then a v5 commit with CPU tests and an FT-off base vs base+fix A/B, >= 4 launches per arm.
